cāvaka makkaḷiṉ pārampariyattuvattaiyum, orumuṟai intap pakutiyil kuṭiyēṟiyiruntataip paṟṟiyum ōraḷavu koñcam kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. intac camaveḷi pāṇṭu nivō nōṭṭō (Pandu Dewo Notto) aḵtāvatu rajuṉōviṉ tantaiyiṉ mukkiya iṭamāka iruntatu. inta aracaṉ mutalil makiḻcciyāka aracāṇṭār. āṉāl tammai uyarttik koḷḷum eṇṇam avar uḷḷattil eḻuntatu. eṉavē avar irācakuruviṉāl (iṟaivaṉāl) viraṭṭap paṭṭār. irācakuru kōpattāl kiḷappiviṭappaṭṭār. intat takāta ceykaiyāl eḻunta kōpa nilaiyāl avar tūkkiyeṟiyap paṭṭu, akṉiyaik kakkum cāṇṭirōṭimōye kōvilil1 avar taṇṭikkap peṟṟār, atēpoḻutu aṅkuḷḷa aintu kōyil kaḷait tavira āṭci muḻuvatum noṟuṅkiyatu. antak kālatti liruntu avar makaṉ muṉvantu, avaṉuṭaiya turppākkiyaṉāṉa tantaiyiṉ niṉaivukkuṟiyāka inta māperum kuṭiyēṟṟattaic ceytu taṉatu iṟuti mūccuḷḷavarai viṭāmuyaṟciyōṭu intak kāṇikkaiyaic celutti vimōcaṉam peṟṟāṉ.
pāṇṭu eṉpavaṉ tēvanōṭṭō, arccuṉaṉ, pīmaṉ ākiya pāṇṭavarkaḷiṉ tantaiyāvāṉ. iṅkuḷḷa kōyilkaḷil oṉṟu pāṇṭuviṉ peyarāl aṭippaṭaiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. oruvēḷai pāṇṭutēvaṉ eṉṟa peyar maruvi paṇṭāṭivō eṉṟu aḻaik kappaṭṭirukkavum kūṭum. pirāhōviṉ (Braho) ucciyil kāṇap paṭum kaṟkuviyal, pirattiyaṭcamāṉa oru paḻaiya īmaviṟakuk kuviyalpōl irukkiṉṟatu. cāvakap paramparai vaḻakkiṟkēṟpa itu pāṇṭuviṉ cāvuc caṭaṅkiṟkuriya viṟakuk kuviyalāka eṇṇap paṭukiṟatu. (intak kuṟukiya nōkkamuḷḷa purāṇak kataiyai iṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ nampuvataṟkillai.)
irāmāyaṇap pāratak kataikaḷukkum tamiḻakattiṟkum evvitac campantamum illai eṉṟālum piṟkālattil uḷḷōr ikkataikaḷōṭu tamiḻakattaip piṇaittu vaittuḷḷaṉar. pāratap pōrukkut tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷiṭam utavi kēṭṭatākavum, cēraṉ utaviyatākavum oru katai. pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉiṉ makaḷ oruttiyai aruccuṉaṉ maṇantatāka maṟṟoru katai. makāpalipuram eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum māmallapurattil uḷḷa pallavarkaḷ oṟṟaikkal kōyil mātirik kōyilāka eḻuppappeṟṟatu. itaṉaip pañca pāṇṭavar iratam - aḵtāvatu tarumarājaratam, pīmaratam arccuṉa ratam, nakularatam, cakātēvaratam eṉṟellām kūṟum kaṭṭukkatai kaḷaip pōl tamiḻarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya cāvakattilum kūṭa intap purāṇak kataikaḷil iṭaic cerukalkaḷaic ceytu cāvakattōṭu iṇaikkappaṭṭiruppatu aṟiñarkaḷ ārāyattakka toṉṟākum. itēpōṉṟu cayāmil irāmakatai pukuntu cayām nāṭṭōṭu pukuntu irāmaṉ cayām nāṭṭāṉ eṉṟum irāmakātai aṅku naṭanta katai eṉṟum cayām maṉṉarkaḷ irāmaṉuṭaiya vaḻit tōṉ ṟalkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭu varuvatum nām cintikkattakkatākum.
ṭaiyeṅ camaveḷiyil catuppu nilattiṉ nilai aṅkuc cellap paṉmuṟai aṭikkaṭi muyaṉṟum tiruptikaramāṉa vaḻi kiṭṭavillai. āṉāl, paṇṭaikkāla intu cakāppattiltāṉ itaṟku nalla vaḻi kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭiruntatu. muṉṉark kuṭiyēṟi irunta iṭattilkūṭa iṉṉum nīrōṭik koṇṭē irukkiṟatu.
kaṭal maṭṭattiṉiṉṟu 6,500 aṭi uyarattil kaviṉ peṟa ilaṅkum ṭaiyeṅ mēṭṭil, aṉṉiyarkaḷukku upayōkamillā mal taṉittup parantu kiṭakkum intak kōyil tokutikaḷ namatu uḷḷattaik kavarak kūṭiya uyarnta iṉpan tarum iṭamākum. tattuvārttam tarum aintu vēṟuvēṟāṉa kōyil tokutikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaikaḷil cila naṉku pātukākkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ēṉaiya vaikaḷ veṟum kaṟkuviyalkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pañca pāṇṭavarkaḷiṉ pukaḻmikka paramparaiyāl aṭikkaṭi ārōpikkum, intiyāvil ātiyil eḻunta paḻamperum kōyil eṉpatu ulakap piracitta peṟṟatākum. itaṟku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkuvatu ceṉṉaik kaṭaṟkaraiyil eṭuppikkap peṟṟa oṟṟaik kalkōyil tokutiyākum avai tarmarāj, pīma, arccuṉaṉ, cakātēva, avarkaḷiṉ potuvāṉa pattiṉiyāṉa turōpatai ākiyavarkaḷiṉ iratamākap peyartarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai namatu cakāptattiṉ ēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērntavai eṉṟu kaṭṭiṭak kalaiyil perum nipuṇarāy viḷaṅkum pēraṟiñar verkūcaṉ avarkaḷ moḻintuḷḷārkaḷ.1
tamiḻ nāṭṭil eḻuntuḷḷa itē eṇṇam cāvakattilum eḻuntu aintu kōyilākap pañcapāṇṭavarkaḷ peyaril kāṇap paṭuvatu āccariyappaṭuvataṟkuriya aruñ ceyalākak karuta muṭiyātu. cāvakattil utiṣṭirarukkuc cāṇṭi pāṇṭatēva eṉṟa peyar vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. cāṇṭipīma, cāṇṭi arccaṉā eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyaruḷḷa kōyilkaḷ eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.
ṭaiyeṅ mēṭu oru nākarikamāṉa talainakarākap pirati palikkavillai. āṉāl puṉitamāṉa teyvīka yāttiraikkuriya iṭamāka miḷirntatu. itu kiṭṭattaṭṭa maturai, tirupparaṅkuṉṟam, kaṉṉiyākumari, tiruccentūr, cīrkāḻi, tirukkaṭaiyūr, citamparam pōṉṟa puṉita iṭamākum. iṅka nirantaramākac camayap purōkitar kaḷum, kōyiṟ paṇiyāḷarkaḷum kuṭiyiruntārkaḷ. iḵtaṉṟip puṉita yāttiraikkārarkaḷ taṅkum iṭaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. āṇṭiṟ korumuṟai iṅkup pārvaiyiṭa varum maṉṉarum ēṉaiyōrum taṅkuvataṟkut takka iṭaṅkaḷumuṇṭu. intak kōyilkaḷ ciṟiyaṉa. ovvoṉṟum cutantiramāṉatu. mikap periyaṉavaṟṟuḷ eṭṭuk kōyilkaḷ maṭṭum ippoḻutu uḷḷaṉa. potuvāka itaṉuṭaiya tōṟṟam peṭṭi pōṉṟatu. allatu ucciyuḷḷatum, ceṅkuttāṉa oḻuṅku muṟaiyaip palamāka vaṟpuṟuttum kaṉacaturamāṉa kaṭṭumā ṉattaiyuṭaiyatumāy irukkiṟatu. ovvoru kōyilum, veḷic cuvariṉ orupakkattiliruntu talaivāyil veḷiyē pitukkik koṇṭu niṟkum allatu oru vāyil maṇṭapattāl neruṅki irukkum ōr uṟutiyāṉa taṉi māṭam irukkum. ēṉaiya mūṉṟu cuvarkaḷiṉ mēṟpuṟaṅkaḷiṉ mēṟpūccukaḷ mūṉṟu pakutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭu mūṉṟu māṭaṅkaḷ allatu ciṟpa araṅkam veḷiyē turuttik koṇṭu irukkum. kūrai piratāṉa māṭattiṉiṉṟu taṉatu amaippait tirumpac ceykiṟatu. uṭpuṟam oru veḷiyāṉa kuḻalvaṭivamāṉa caricamattiṇmam uṭaiyatāyk, kūraiyiṉ uḷ tuḷaiyuḷḷa kūmpuvaṭivap poruḷiṟkuk kīḻē, oru taṉik kallāl mūṭak kūṭiyatāṉa eñciya iṭaṅkaḷ varaikkum uṭcuvarkaḷ neruṅki irukkiṉṟaṉa. oru vicittiramāṉa uruvan tāṅkiya kīrtti mukam (kalā makaramum, ulāntā āciriyarkaḷiṉ pāṉā pakutiyum) vāyilaiyum, māṭaṅkaḷaiyum aṇiceytu niṟkiṉṟatu. makaram apivirutti ceyyappaṭṭum, malar uruvaṅkaḷāl oppaṉai ceyyappaṭṭum aritāṉateṉa aṟiñarkaḷāl aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭu muḷḷatu.
kalā makarā
kalā makarā eṉpatu vāyilkaḷaiyum māṭaṅkaḷiṉ muṉpuṟaṅ kaḷaiyum alaṅkarittuk kāṭṭa oru vicittiramāṉa vilaṅku vaṭivaic ceṭikaḷālum koṭikaḷālum ceytu kaṭṭum ōr aḻakiya tōraṇamēyākum.
mēṟṟaḷam oṉṟiliruntu maṟṟoru mēṟṟaḷattiṉ vaḻiyāka mattiya ṭakōpāviṟkuc cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ nāṉku ticai tirumpāk kūṟāka mukkiyamāṉa kuṟiyāka irukkiṟatu. atu teḷi vākak kōyiliṉ puṉitap poruḷkaḷiṉ puṉitamāy irukkiṟatu. mēṟṟaḷattiṉ uṭpukavirukkum makattāṉa periya nuḻaivāyil ovvoṉṟum aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. amaippu muṟaikaḷ evvāṟu vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum mattiya intiyāviṉ muṟkāla tūpaṅkaḷiṉ pukaḻpeṟṟa tōraṇaṅkaḷiṉ alaṅkarippu muṟaiyai aṭuttup pōrōputūr, iṉṉum eṇṇaṟṟa kōyilkaḷiṉ vāyilkaḷiṉ mēl uḷḷa tōraṇa alaṅkarippu muṟai cantēkattiṟ kiṭamiṉṟi intiya mūlattai uṭaiyatāka irukkiṉṟatu. mika vicittiramāṉa intap periya nuḻaivāyilkaḷiṉ ciṟanta iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉa talaivāyil mēlē alaṅkāramāka amaikkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. atu payaṅkaramāṉa teyvamākiya kālāviṉ mātiri uruva māka matikkappaṭukiṟatu, intiyāvil inta alaṅkāra vēlaip pāṭukaḷ ellām oru yāḷiyiṉ talaiyai vaittē aṇi ceyyap paṭum. tekkāṇattil uḷḷa tirāviṭak kaṭṭiṭak kalaiyil iṉṉum yāḷiyiṉ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu tirāviṭak kaṭṭiṭak kalaiyaippaṟṟi naṉku ārāynta ciṟpaviṟpaṉṉarum pirañcup pēraṟiñarumāṉa jāvē tuparē avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.1 ippoḻutu kālā talai eṉṟa kūṟappaṭum oru putiya uruvattaic cāvakac ciṟpikaḷ cāvaka nāṭṭuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟilum iṇaittu alaṅkarittuḷḷaṉar. iḵtu intiyāvil ciṟpikaḷ vāyilkaḷiṉ irupuṟamum vaittu aṇi ceyyum makarattalai pōl cāvakac ciṟpikaḷ itaip payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar.
kalā - makarā ciṟappuk kūṟu ōr iṇaikkap peṟṟa alaṅkarippu eṉṟu ulāntā putaiporuḷ ārāycci aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. iḵtu intō - cāvakak kalaiyiṉ alaṅkaripput tiṭṭaṅkaḷil mikac ciṟantatākat tikaḻkiṟatu. intiyac ciṟpakkalai yil kūṭa makaram ōr āpūrvamāṉa ciṟpap poruḷākak karutappaṭu kiṟatu. āṉāl ataṉuṭaiya mūlat tōṟṟattaip paṟṟi māṟupaṭṭa karuttukaḷ aṟiñarkaḷiṭaiyē tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. ataṉuṭaiya curuṇṭa tutikkai yāṉaiyaic cuṭṭuvatāka irukkum. āṉāl intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil makaram eṉpatu māṟṟak kūṭāta cāyal kāṭṭum nīril vāḻum oru mirukampōl irukkum. nām makara kālā mukāviṉ poḻutu pōkkukkuriya vinōtak kataiyai niṉaivukūra muṭikiṟatu. atōṭu pañcatantirak kataikaḷil irākkatamukak kuraṅkaiyum, pauttarkaḷ paṭippiṉaikkāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭa cātakak kataiyaiyum niṉaivupaṭutta muṭikiṟatu.2 nīril vāḻum makara mīṉaiyum nilattil vāḻum yāṉaiyaiyum iṇaittu oru vinōtamāṉa pirāṇiyaic ciṟpavaṭivil kaṇṭataip pērāciriyar kuruṉ veṭal eṉpavar nīr yāṉai eṉṉum peyarāl kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.1 eṉṟālum makara uruvam muṉṉar intiyak kalaikaḷil oru vilaṅ kākavē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu mutalai vaṭivu oru vicēṭa alaṅkārap paṭuttappaṭṭu eṅkum evarum kaṇṭirāta oru putiya uruvam koṭuk kappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. vaṭa intiyāvil pīkāril uḷḷa lōma muṉi kukaiyil makaram ataṉ paḻaṅkāla uruvil cittarikkappaṭṭirup patai nām pārkka muṭikiṟatu. intiyāviṉ paḻaṅkālak kukaikaḷil kaṇṭa iyāṉa vaṭivamuḷḷa mirukattiṉiṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāka urumāṟṟamuṟṟu iṟutiyākac cāvakak kalaiyiṉ vicittirap pāṇiyil amainta vicittirap paṭaippuyirāka eḻuntatu. aḵtu aṭuttu oru putiya amaippaip peṟṟu apiviruttiyuṟṟut tōraṇa mākak kāṇappeṟṟatu. ittōraṇaṅkaḷ mattiya intiyāvil pārhūttiluḷḷa pukaḻ vāynta tūpāvil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. atiluḷḷa iru nuṉikaḷum mūvitamāṉa tūṇiṉ ucciyiluḷḷa paṟkaṭṭaiyiṉ mītuḷḷa pakutiyil makara uruvattōṭu iṇaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum aṉaittum mutalaiyiṉuṭaiya mūlat taṉmaikaḷ māṟāmalum aḻiyāmalum eñci niṟkiṉṟaṉa.2
eṉavē ivaikaḷaiyellām naṉkārāynta aṟiñar ṭākṭar. cē. piyec vōkal avarkaḷ tam nūlil potuvākac cāvakattil kāṇappaṭum kalā makara alaṅkāra vēlaippāṭukaḷ niccayamāka intiyak kalaiyaic cārntaṉavē eṉpatai uṟutip paṭuttiyuḷḷār. pōrōputūr puṉita ālayattiṉ periya nuḻai vāyiliṉmītu cittirikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kalāmakara tōraṇa vēlaip pāṭukaḷ intiyac ciṟpa nuṭpaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu ciṟitu māṟupaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭiṉum intiya mūlavaṭiviṉiṉṟu apiviruttiyuṟṟuc cāvakap paṇpōṭu kalantuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟalām eṉa naṉku viḷakki irukkiṉṟār.3
intak kalāmakara tōraṇa ciṟpam mattiya cāvakattil mika mukkiyattuvam peṟṟatāka irukkiṟatu. itu kiḻakkuc cāvakattiṉ ciṉṉamākavum viḷaṅkiyatu. mattiya cāvakattilum kiḻakkuc cāvakat tilum kāṇappaṭum kalāmakara alaṅkāra vēlaippāṭukaḷ aṭip paṭaiyil cila vēṟupāṭukaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉa. mēlum kiḻakkuc cāvakat til atikamākak kāṇappaṭuvatillai. āṉāl aṅku itaṟku atika matippu aḷikkiṉṟaṉar.1
iṅku nām kāṭṭiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ cāvakattil uḷḷa arccuṉaṉ tokuti eṉṟa nāṉku kōyilkaḷum muṟaiyē cāṇṭi arccuṉā, cirikāṇṭi, pāṇṭatēvā, cempatrā eṉappaṭum. cāṇṭi kāṭṭē kācā eṉappaṭuvatu nīṇṭa caturamuṭaiya ciṟiya cāṇṭi cemar āka muṭiyātu. oruvēḷai ārampattil pokkica cālaiyāka iruntu appāl arccuṉaṉ tokutiyil oru pākamāka itai amaittiruk kalām. taṉittu oṟṟaiyāka uyarntu niṟkum cāṇṭi pīmā ivaikaḷiṉiṉṟu muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭatākak kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu. intak kaṭṭiṭattiṉ kīḻppakuti nām ēṟkeṉavē vivaritta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷai yoppa irukkiṟatu. āṉāl kūrai teḷivākap paṭṭaik kūmpu vaṭivuṭaiyatāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. atu mēṟpūccōṭu aṭittaḷattiṉ amaippu mutalilēyē tirumpat tirumpac ceyyappaṭṭu irukkiṟatu. ataṉuṭaiya araṅkaṅkaḷ kuṟaittuc ceṅkuttāṉatākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟaiyavai talaiyai oṭṭic caittiya palakaṇi pūvaṇi vēlaikaḷ uṭaiyaṉavāy alaṅkarikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa, allatu piratimaikaḷ uyarnta ātaravuḷḷaṉavāy nāṉkāvatu āṟāvatu nilaikaḷiṉ kōṇaṅkaḷāka mutirnta tāmaraik kāykaḷāl nirappappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ōr aḻakiya taṉmaiyil oru niṟaivāṉa tāmaraikkāy ucciyai irājarīkam ceykiṟatu. ivvāṟu kūrai amaippu intō - āriya cikara amaippai oppa irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kalai mētaikaḷ cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. itu tañcāvūriluḷḷa irācarācēccurattaip pōl mika apivirutti yaṭainta nilaiyil mika nerukkamuṭaiyatākat tulaṅkukiṟatu.
ṭaiyaṅmēṭu pala cilaikaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭākap peṟ ṟirukkiṟatu. cāṇṭi cirikāṇṭiyiṉ pirivil uḷḷa viṣṇu (tirumāl), nāṉmukaṉ civaṉ mutaliya uruvaṅkaḷ ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭu kaḷākum. ataṉ ciṟpavēlaippāṭukaḷ nuṭpam vāyntu api viruttiyaṭaintuḷḷaṉa. cāṇṭi pīmāviṉ māṭattiluḷḷa caittiyap palakaṇiyiṉ ucci amaippu mikak kavarcci taruvaṉavākum. itu ciṟpiyiṉ conta muyaṟciyiṉ payaṉāka eḻuntatākum. itaṉ talai, cāvakakkalaiyiṉ purātaṉa muṟaiyait taḻuviyatāka iruntālum alaṅkāra vēlaippāṭukaḷum ciṉṉaṅkaḷum muṟṟilum porutta māka illai eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka uḷḷaṉa.
ṭaiyaṅmēṭṭiṉ kiḻakkilum teṟkilum eṇṇaṟṟa ciṟu kōyilkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷ ellām aṭippaṭai yil itē muṟaiyil amaikkappaṭṭavaikaḷākum. āṉāl caṟṟut tārāḷamākavum aṭikkaṭi vicittiraṅkaḷ kāṭṭum muṟaiyilum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. campiṅmalai arukil uḷḷa cāṇṭi cikiriyum ki.pi. 850 ām āṇṭil arumpiya caiva cāṇṭipiriṅ kāppa eṉpatum eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mika mukkiyamāṉa kōyil keṭāṅ caṅkā eṉappaṭum, uṅkāraṉiṉ kōyilkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷākum. avaikaḷ oṉpatu ciṟu kōyilkaḷiṉ tokutikaḷāka aṭaṅki malai ucciyil amaintuḷḷaṉa. avai nalla puṉita yāttirai mārkkattil irukkiṉṟaṉa.
ippoḻutu ṭaiyaṅ kōyilkaḷ appoḻutuḷḷa peyar  kaḷil vaḻaṅkap peṟātu cāṇṭi pīmā, cāṇṭi arccuṉā eṉṟa peyarkaḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuvaruvatu nāṭōṭi makkaḷiṉ kataiyai aṉucarittup pārkkil atu mikavum kavarcciyākavē irukkum. āṉāl ataṉ varalāṟṟaiyum mūla uṟpattiyaiyum  uṇarvataṟku evvitat tuṇaiyum utaviyum taramuṭiyātu. aṅkuḷḷa pala paṭimaṅ kaḷum caiva camayappaṭimaṅkaḷākavē terikiṉṟaṉa. avai oru kālattil civaperumāṉai vaṇaṅkuvataṟkāka arppaṇikkappaṭ ṭirukka vēṇṭum. ittīvakattil ikkalai putta camayattōṭu arukarukē intu camayamuṟaiyum pirapalamāyc cāvaka varalāṟṟil intu cakāptam muḻuvatilum vaḷarntu vantuḷḷatu. nāḷāvaṭṭat til iru camaya neṟikaḷum viyakkattakka vitamākak kūṭi iruntiruk kiṉṟaṉa. cāvakattil civaperumāṉ palvēṟu muṟaiyil vaṇaṅkap paṭṭālum pattirakuru eṉṟa peyaril peritum pōṟṟappaṭṭār. avar yōkiyāy, tāṭiyum, tacaippaṟṟumuṭaiyavarāyk kaiyil kamaṇṭalamum jepamālaiyum uṭaiyavarāyk kāṇappaṭṭār.1
mattiya cāvakattil cumattirarkaḷiṉ āṭciyil (ki.pi. 732 mutal 860) - aḵtāvatu cailēntira marapiṉar āṭciyil arumpiya pala mukkiya niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷai nām ippoḻutu ālōcikka vēṇṭum. 778ām āṇṭil eḻunta cāṇṭikkalacāṉ cāvaka maṇṇil talai tūkkiya pautta niṉaivuc ciṉṉamāṉatu mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya vilaimatikka voṇṇāta mukkiyamāṉa nikaḻcciyākum. aṅkuk kiṭaitta cācaṉam mūlam cailēntira maṉṉaṉ arppaṇitta tārā tēviyiṉ tiruuruvam mattiya maṇṭa pattil iṭam peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm intak kōyil perampāṉaṉ kōyilukku mēṟkup pakkattil iṭam peṟṟiruntatu. inta mēṭu atika nerukkamāṉa makkaḷ vāḻum nilapparappāka iruntatu. oru mukkiyamāṉa talainakarattiṉ pakutiyāka allatu mattiya cāvaka āṭcik kālam muḻuvatum talainakaramāka iruntatu. ṭaiyaṅ mātiriyil irukkum cāṇṭik kalacāṉ putuppippataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum taṉi vāyil uḷḷa pakkak kōyilkaḷāka apivirutti aṭaintu arukē puṭaittirukkum māṭaṅkaḷaip peṟṟiruntatu. piramāṇṭamāṉa kīrttimukā eṉṉum kalāmakarat tōraṇam piratāṉa vāyilaiyum māṭaṅkaḷaiyum aṇi ceytatu. makaratōraṇa vaḷaivukaḷ kīḻē muḻuvatum vicittiramāṉa ciṟpa vēlaippāṭukaḷ niṟaintaṉavāka māṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. cuvarkaḷ cuvaimikka paṭṭaikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭaṉa.

10. aṉṉiyar āṭciyil āyvu
iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākac (ki.pi. 1619 mutal 1798 varai) cāvakam ṭaccu, kiḻakkintiyak kumpiṉi āṭciyil iruntu vantatu. inta aṉṉiya āṭciyāḷarkaḷ iṅku vaṇikak kaṇṇōṭṭat tōṭu pukuntu piṉṉarc cāvaka nāṭṭaik kavarntu atai aṭimaip paṭutti āṇṭu vantaṉar. anta nāṭṭai iyaṉṟa aḷavu curaṇṭik koṇṭu vantaṉar eṉṟālum inta vaṇika maṉappāṉmai koṇṭa veḷḷaiyarkaḷ aṅkup paravik kiṭanta nākarikattaiyum niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷaiyum kōyilkaḷaiyum aḻipāṭukaḷaiyum mikka āccariyattōṭu pārttu vantaṉar. patiṉeṭṭāvatu nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutivarai avarkaḷiṭam oru vitapparivu uṇarcciyō allatu oru teḷivāṉa viḷakkamō arumpavillai. āṉāl oru mēleḻunta vāriyāṉa āpūrvamāṉa kaṇṇōṭṭam apivirutti aṭaintu kalviyuṇarcciyāka eḻuntu āḻnta ārāycciyil iṟaṅkat tūṇṭiyatu. 1778 ām āṇṭu paṭṭō viyā viññāṉak kalaikkaḻakam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ataṉuṭaiya nōkkam vicayaṅkaḷai ārāyvatu maṭṭumaṉṟu, vivacāyam, vaṇikam intak kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭṭiṉ celvac ceḻippil akkarai celuttuvatu mākum. āṉāl, atōṭu iyaṟkai varalāṟu, paṇṭaikkāla niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ, makkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷ ākiyavaikaḷaiyum āytalākum.
ivaikaḷaip paṟṟi mutaṉmutalāka akkarai koṇṭu cāvakattiṉ paḻamperum ciṉṉaṅkaḷai āya muṉ vantavarkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavar aṅku 1801 ām āṇṭu mutal 1808 ām āṇṭu varai vaṭakiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkaraik kavarṉarāka irunta nikkōla eṅkalhārṭ eṉpavarākum. avaratu tūṇṭutaliṉ mītu peram pāṉaṉ aḻipāṭukaḷum iṉṉum pala kōyilkaḷum cuttappaṭṭup paricōtikkappaṭṭaṉa. ṭaccu kavarṉar intap paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ ārāycci ārvattāl atikārappūrmākap pala arumperum paṭimaṅkaḷaiyellām kōyiliṉiṉṟu avaratu cemrāṅ (Semarang) eṉṉum iṭattil nāṭṭuppuṟa illamākiya ṭi varuca hiṭ eṉṉum iṭattiṟkuk koṇṭu pōka uttaraviṭṭār. appāl ivaikaḷellām ulāntā nāṭṭiluḷḷa leyṭaṉ nakariluḷḷa maṉita iṉap parappup paṟṟiya āyviyal (Ethnography) paḻam poruḷ kāṭciccālaiyil iṭampeṟṟaṉa.
appāl piriṭṭiṣ āṭciyiṉ celvākkuc cāvakattil vaḷarntiruntapoḻutu (1811 - 16) ciṟanta aṟivāḷiyāyum, aracap piratinitiyāyum (Governor - General) āka viḷaṅkiyavar car ṭāmpōrṭu rēval (Sir. Stamford Raffles) eṉpavar āvar. avar varalāṟṟu ārāycci, putaiporuḷ ārāycci ākiya iruperun tuṟaikaḷi lum akkālattirunta evaraiyum viṭac ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉār. avaratu ātaravil cāvakattil paḻam poruḷ ārāycci toṭarntu periya aḷavil naṭaipeṟṟu vantatu. ataṉ viḷaivākac cāvakattiṉ varalāṟu (laṇṭaṉ 1817) eṉṟa arumperum nūl veḷivantatu. intac ciṟanta nūl veḷivantataṉ mūlam rēvalkku mikunta kauravam vantatu. cāvakap putaiporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟaiyil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa iṉṟaiya māṇavarkaḷukkumkūṭa avaratu nūl mikavum payaṉuḷḷatāka irukkiṟatu. āṉāl varalāṟṟup pakutiyil perumpālāṉavai nampakkūṭiya takavalkaḷ alla - avaikaḷ nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷiṉ kuṟippukaḷiṉiṉṟum cācaṉaṅkaḷiṉ mikaippaṭuttik kūṟappaṭṭa vācakaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum eḻutappaṭṭavai kaḷākum. eṉavē avaikaḷai mika eccarikkaiyōṭu eṭuttāḷa vēṇṭum. avarukkup piṉ vanta āciriyar jēm perkūcaṉ (James Fergusson) eṉpavar āvar. avar rēval nūlai nampi, varalāṟu campantamāṉa takavalkaḷai meyyeṉa nampit tam nūlil pala iṭaṅ kaḷil mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭip perun tavaṟukku āṭpaṭṭār.
paḻamperuñ cāvakattiṉ niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷaiyum kōyil kaḷaiyum ārāya virumpip pala aṟiñarkaḷ tamatu vilaimatikka oṇṇā kālattaiyum caktiyaiyum kaṭanta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil celaviṭṭuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷuḷ palar ṭaccu nāṭṭaic cērntavarka ḷāvārkaḷ. avarkaḷ annāṭṭiṉ palkalaik kaḻakattil payiṉṟu uyarnta kalaippaṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟṟavarkaḷāvārkaḷ. avarkaḷil nāṉ iṅkuk kuṟippiṭattakkavar pērāciriyar heṇṭirik kerṉ (Hendrik Kern) avarukku vaṭamoḻiyilum  cāvaka moḻiyilum nalla pulamaiyuṇṭu. cāvakattiṉ paṇṭaikkāla nūlkaḷ eḻutap peṟṟirunta kāvi moḻiyaiyum avar kaṟṟiruntār. cāvakattil vaṭamoḻiyilum, kāvi moḻiyilum varaiyappaṭṭa cācaṉaṅkaḷai viyakkattakkavāṟu avar patippittuḷḷār. vēṟu pala aṟivāḷi kaḷiṉ nūṟkaḷōṭu aṟiñar kerṉ eḻutiya paḻaiya cāvaka irāmā yaṇamum patippikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉoṭu ataṉ ṭaccu moḻi peyarppum veḷi vantuḷḷatu.
aṟiñar kerṉ eṉpavariṉ tiṟamai mikka māṇavar ṭākṭar pirāṇṭa eṉpavarākum. avar intōṉēciya āṭcipparappil vāḻpavar. kalaittuṟaiyilum putaiporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟaiyi lum naṉku payiṟci peṟṟavar. paḻaiya moḻikaḷilum navīṉa moḻi kaḷilum ariya aṟivuttiṟam peṟṟavar. cāvaka niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅ kaḷai ārāyvataṟkākap paṭṭēviya aracāṅkattāl niyamaṉañ ceyyappeṟṟavar. avar eḻutiya iraṇṭu taṉipperum nūlkaḷ - aḵtāvatu oṉṟu jākōviṉ kōyilaip paṟṟiyatu (1904). maṟṟatu avar iṟanta piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷākiya ciṅkācāriyaip paṟṟi yum paṉatāraṉaip paṟṟiyatumākum (1909). irumātiri nūlkaḷum aḻakiya paṭaṅkaḷōṭu iyaṉṟa aḷavu muyaṉṟu niṟaivuṭaiya nūlāka veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa.
1905 ām āṇṭu aṟiñar pirāṇṭa avarkaḷiṉ akāla maraṇattiṟkup piṉ aintu āṇṭukaḷ uruṇṭōṭiṉa. appāl avaratu iṭattil ṭākṭar. eṉ. jē. kurōm niyamikkap peṟṟār. avar oru putaiporuḷ ārāyccik kuḻuvai nirmāṇittu ārāyntu vantār.
appērārāycciyiṉ payaṉākac cāvakattiṉ paḻaiya kalai, varalāṟu, kalveṭṭu ākiyavaikaḷaip paṟṟip pala arum perum ārāyccik kaṭṭuraikaḷ veḷivantaṉa. appāl avar 1915 ām āṇṭil ōyvu peṟṟa piṉṉar intu cāvakakkalaiyiṉ kainūl1 (Hand Book of Hindu Javaness Art) eṉṟa ōr uyarnta nūlai eḻuti iraṇṭu tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭār.
annūlkaḷ niṟaiyat tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa takavalkaḷaiyum paṭaṅkaḷaiyum uṭaiyaṉavāka iruntaṉa. pōrōputūriṉ talai ciṟanta niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷāl cāvakattiṉ putaiporuḷkaḷai viḷakkum mākṉam āpa (Magnum opus); 444 periya pakkaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa uyarnta vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa tāḷkaḷil mikac ciṟanta aḻakiya niḻaṟpaṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa ciṟanta aḻakiya nūlāka veḷivantatu.2
1919 ām āṇṭu ṭicampar mātam ṭākṭar kurōm leyṭaṉ palkalaik kaḻakattil cāvakap putaiporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟaik kāka ārampikkappaṭṭa putiya iṭattiṟkup pērāciriyarāy niyamaṉañ ceyyappaṭṭār. avarukkup piṉ anta iṭattiṟku vantavar cāvakattiṉ putai poruḷ ārāyccittuṟait talaivarāy irunta ṭākṭar ve.ṭi.kē. pōc eṉpavarāvār. avarukku oru ciṟiya utavi nipuṇarum iruntu vantār. ippoḻutu avarē paḻam poruḷkaḷaip pātukāttal ārāytal eṉṉum iruperum paṇikaḷaiyum naṭatti varukiṉṟār. avaratu ciṟanta paṇi atikāri kaḷukkum aracāṅkattiṟkum perum matippai aḷittuḷḷatu. itaṉ mūlam cāvakattil eḻunta intu nākarikattiṉ ciṟappu ulāntāvil maṭṭumiṉṟi airōppā muḻuvatilum paraviyatu.
cāvakattil uḷḷa makkaḷum iṉṟuvarai viḻippuṟṟuc cāvakattil pīṭuṟa niṉṟa kalaik karuvūlattai ārāyamuṉvaravillai. āṉāl cumār nāṟpatu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ vaṅkāḷattil māperum intiyakkaḻakam (Greater India Society) eṉṟa oru niṟuvaṉam kaṇṭu ciṟitu kālam cāvakak kalaikaḷaippaṟṟi ārāycci ceyya muyaṉṟaṉar. anta uṇarcci nīṇṭanāḷ nilaittu niṟkavillai. iṉṟu cāvaka aracāṅkam mulīm aracāṅkamāy iruppatāl intuk kōyil kaḷaiyum, paṭimaṅkaḷaiyum paṟṟi ārāyvataṟku akkarai koḷḷa villai. intiya aracāṅkam matac cārpaṟṟa aracāṅkamāy iruppa tāl cāvakattil nilaviya intuk kalaikaḷaippaṟṟi ārāyamuṟpaṭu vatu aracāṅkattiṉ mūlakkoḷkaikku māṟāka irukkum eṉṟu eṇṇik koṇṭirukkalām eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
piriṭṭaṉ, pirañcu aracāṅkaṅkaḷ kiṟitava aracāṅkaṅ kaḷāka iruntālum cāvakattiluḷḷa intiyak kalaikaḷil akkarai celuttiyatu kaṇṭu nām peritum pārāṭṭa vēṇṭiyavarāy irukkiṉṟōm. iṅkilāntu pōṉṟa nāṭṭiliruntu avarkaḷiṉ kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭāka irunta intiyā, ilaṅkai, malēciyā, ciṅkappūr, intōṉēciyā, intō cīṉā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku vanta putai poruḷ ārāyccit tuṟaiyiṉar maṭṭumiṉṟi āṭci ceyta kōṭṭattalaivar āḷunar pōṉṟavarkaḷum taṅkaḷiṉ aracāṅkap paṇiyōṭamaiyātu, itara kalait tuṟaikaḷilum akkarai celuttip perumpaṇi ceytu vantiruppatu nammāl eṉṟum maṟakkaṟpāla taṉṟu. iṉṟaiya cutantira intiyāviṉ piratinitikaḷāyum tūtuvar kaḷāyum, vaṇikak kuḻuviṉarkaḷāyum, kāriyataricikaḷāyum iruppa varkaḷ anta nāṭṭil uḷḷa intiyak kalaikaḷaiyum nākarikattaip paṟṟiyum cintikka vēṇṭum.
iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar ki.pi. 1746il mattiya cāvakattil uḷḷa perampāṉaṉil aracappirati nitiyāka (kavarṉar ceṉaralāka) iruntavar vāṉ imhōv eṉpavar ākum. avar perampāṉaṉ pakutiyil uḷḷa tolporuḷkaḷaik kaṇṭukaḷikka virumpi aṅkuc ceṉṟatākak cāvakattaippaṟṟi mutaṉmutalāka eḻutiya āciriyar oruvar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. aṅku aracappiratiniti kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟil turkkaiyiṉ veṇkalap paṭimamum oṉṟu. ataik kaṇṭatum avarukku aṭiyil varum eṇṇam eḻuntatu. atu, cāvakarkaḷ muṉṉar yūtarkaḷō, kiṟitavarkaḷō, ilāmiyarkaḷō, illāta puṟac camayattārkaḷiṭaiyē yuḷḷa pirāmaṇa vakuppiṉarāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatēyākum. āṅkilēya atikārikaḷ 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ārampattilum, intiyāvil cēvai ceytapiṉ cāvakattiṟku māṟṟappaṭṭa piṉṉarum intiyāvilum cāvakattilumuḷḷa niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅ kaḷaip paṟṟi ārāya muṟpaṭṭaṉar eṉpatu kuṟittu āccariyap paṭuvataṟkillai. intiyāviṟkum cāvakattiṟkumuḷḷa toṭarpu kaḷaiyum vēṟupala ceytikaḷaiyum paṟṟi avarkaḷ nuṇuki ārāyn tuḷḷaṉar. car. ulliyam jōṉ, mārṭaṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷ intōṉēciya moḻikaḷiluḷḷa vaṭamoḻip pataṅkaḷaippaṟṟi yellām ārāyntu vantaṉar. uylhelm vāṉ hampōlṭ (Wilhelm Von Humbolt) eṉpavar eḻutiya periya nūlākiya upar ṭai kāvi - pirāc (Ulber die Kauvi - Sprache) eṉpatu avar iṟantapiṉ ki.pi. 1836 ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu.1 atil, cāvakattiṉ varalāṟu campantappaṭṭa ātāraṅkaḷai ārāya muṟpaṭṭāl eṇṇaṟṟa cācaṉaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam intiyarkaḷiṉ celvākku avar kaḷiṉ moḻi, eḻuttu, kalai, camayaṅkaḷ mūlam paravi iruntaṉa eṉpatait teḷivāka aṟiya muṭiyum. cāvakattiṉ varalāṟu campanta māka eḻutappeṟṟa iru nūlkaḷ cāvakattil uḷḷaṉa. avai paḻaiya cāvaka moḻi allatu kāvi moḻiyil uḷ nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟu putaiporuḷ ārāycci mutaliyavaikaḷaippaṟṟi varaiyap paṭṭaṉavākum. avaikaḷil oṉṟāṉa nākarakirēṭṭakāmā (Nagarakratagam) eṉpatu 1894 ām āṇṭu lōmpōk paṭai yeḻucciyiṉ pōtu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭatu. aḵtu iṟutiyāka irunta pālit tīviṉ talaivaṉ tī mūṭṭiya poḻutu intak kai yeḻuttup pirati ṭākṭar pirāṇṭa eṉpavarāl kāppāṟṟap paṭṭatu. inta nūl eppaṭi iruppiṉum atu ki.pi. 1365 ām āṇṭu niṟaivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. irunūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākak kiḻakkuc cāvakattil āṭcipurinta marapiṉarkaḷaip paṟṟiyatu. itil intiyā viṟkum cāvakattiṟkumuḷḷa paṇṭaikkālat toṭarpukaḷ evaiyum kāṇappaṭavillai.
cīṉanāṭṭu vaḻippōkkaṉ varainta payaṇak kuṟippukkaḷilum cīṉa aracāṅkattiṉ kālakkiramamāṉa varalāṟṟilum kāṇak kiṭakkum takavalkaḷaik koṇṭum niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṭṭum cila cāṉṟukaḷaik koṇṭum  intiya - cāvakat toṭarpukaḷai oruvāṟu aṟiyalām. paḻaṅkālattil vanta paricutta payaṇikaḷil pāhiyaṉ (Fa Hien) eṉpavar ilaṅkaiyiṉiṉṟu cīṉam pōntāṉ. puyalāl avaṉatu kappal tākkappaṭṭu allalkaḷukku āḷāki (avaṉ kuṟippiṭum) ī - pō - ti (Ye - Po - Ti) eṉṉum iṭattai aṭaintāṉ. atu cāvakam allatu cumantirāvāka irukka vēṇṭum.
avar, inta nāṭṭil ēṟpamaiti illāta kōṭpāṭṭil paṟṟuṭaiyavarkaḷum, purōkita matattil nampikkaiyuṭaiyavar kaḷum niṟaintirukkiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl putta pakavāṉiṉ vitikaḷ avarkaḷukku atikam teriyātu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ivar tarum takaval mūlam ki.pi. 400 ām āṇṭil iṅkuc caivacamaya nākarikam paravi niṉṟatu. pautta matam peritum kāṇappaṭavillai eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ivar caiva neṟiyai ataṉ caṭaṅkācāraṅkaḷaik koṇṭu purōkita matam eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. ivar tarum takaval varalāṟu campantappaṭṭa ōr uṟutiyāṉa ceytiyākum.
appāl ki.pi. 671 ām āṇṭu maṟṟōr cīṉa yāttiraik kārar iṭciṅ (I - tsing) intiyāviṟkuc cellum poḻutu ḵpō - ccē (Fo - che) eṉṟa iṭattaik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. atu pālampāṅ eṉṟa cumattirāt tīvil uḷḷa iṭamākum. aṅku appoḻutu puttamatam naṉṟākap paravi iruntatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ḵpō - ccē intu nākarikattiṉ maiya nilaiyamākak kāṇappaṭṭatu. eṉiṉum aṅku avar vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇam payilum poruṭṭu āṟu mātam taṅkiyatāka avaruṭaiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mūlam terikiṟatu.
pirañcup pērāciriyar ṭākṭar jiyārja kōyiṭṭa mēṟkuc cāvaka nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟil ki.pi. 500 ām āṇṭu mutal 1300ām āṇṭu varai putaintu kiṭanta  arumperum nikaḻccikaḷai yellām ārāyntu ulakiṟku uṇarttiṉār. ivaratu ārāycci veḷivarumuṉ mēṟkuc cāvakattaip paṟṟi oru ciṟitutāṉ ulakattiṟkut teriyum. malāyt tīpakaṟpattiṉ mēṟkup pakutiyil valimai mikka intu malāy kappaṟpaṭai veṟṟimuracu koṭṭi niṉṟatu. inta āṭciyē srī vijaya āṭci eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. atu cailēntira araca marapiṉarkku uriyatāka iruntatu. intat tīpakaṟpat tiṉ varalāṟu vēṟu enta varalāṟṟaiyum viṭak kavarccimikkatāyum niṉaittāl perum uḷḷakkiḷarcciyai ūṭṭuvatākavum uḷḷatu. aṟiñar kōyiṭṭa ārāycci veḷivantu ulakiluḷḷa pala aṟiñarkaḷiṉ uḷḷattai īrttatu. eṉavē avaratu ārāycciyai uṟutippaṭuttavum avarāl viṭappaṭṭa pala nikaḻccikaḷai mēṟ koṇṭu ārāyavum ataṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷait tēṭavum, muṉṉark kaṇṭa pala ceytikaḷōṭu putitāyk kaṇṭa uṇmaikaḷai iṇaittum cāvaka varalāṟṟai oru niṟaivāṉa varalāṟākat tikaḻum paṭi ceyya muyaṟci eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. kōyiṭṭa1 avarkaḷait tavira kurōyeṉvelṭ (Groeneveldt)1 pelliyaṭ (Pelliot)2 perrāṇṭ (Ferrand)3 kurōm (Krom)4 ṭaṭṭā. heym (Stutterheim)5, macumtār (Mazumdar) II,6 piricilaki (Przyluski)7 pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ cāvaka varalāṟṟai aṇuki ārāyntu intak kālakaṭṭa varalāṟṟiṟkup putiya veḷiccattai aḷittaṉar. iḵtaṉṟi itaṟkup piṉ aṇmaik kālattil pala aṟiñarkaḷ cāvaka varalāṟṟai mēṟkoṇṭum ārāyntu atai apivirutti ceyya muṉvantaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai muṉkūṟiya aṟiñarkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalōṭu iṇaittuk koḷvatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. avarkaḷuḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavar jē.el. mōyaṉ eṉpavarākum. avar ittīpakaṟpattiṉ varalāṟṟil mutal āyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ virivāṉa maṟumalarcciyai ārāya muyaṉṟuḷḷār. ivar cāvaka nāṭṭuc ceytikaḷaippaṟṟi ārāynta varalāṟṟu māṇavarkaḷil ciṟappumikkavarākum. avar kaiyāṇṭa vaḻikaḷāl tikaipput tarum mukkiyamāṉa ceytikaḷ atikam illāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu. avarukku muṉ ārāynta aṟiñar kaḷaip pōlaṉṟi mikak kaṇṭippāṉa uḷḷattiṉ vivēkamuḷḷa muṟaiyaip peṟṟiruntār. intap pakutikaḷil payaṇañ ceyta paṇṭaikkāla vaḻippōkkarkaḷāṉa arēpiyarkaḷ cīṉarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷiṉ kuṟippukkaḷiliruntu tokukkap peṟṟa nila iyal paṟṟiya ceytikaḷiṉ tokuti aṟiñar mōyaṉsiṉ vātattiṟku mukkiyamāṉa aṭippaṭaiyāyiruntatu.
cāvaka nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟaippaṟṟiyum makkaḷiṉ nampikkaikaḷ, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, kalaikaḷiṉ maṟumalarcci ākiyavaikaḷaip paṟṟiyum tiru. mōyaṉ avarkaḷiṉ tiṟamaimikka ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāy uṇmaiyāṉa nilai ulakiṟku aṟivikkappaṭṭatu.
avaratu ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāka intu - malēya āṭci malēciyat tīpakaṟpattil niṟuvappaṭṭiruntatu eṉṟu uṟutip paṭṭatu. tamiḻakattil naṭaipeṟṟa pōrkaḷiṉ payaṉāka iṅkup pala nikaḻccikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. veṟṟi peṟṟavarkaḷ malēyat tīpakaṟpattil pala akatikaḷaik kuṭiyēṟṟiṉar. intak  kōṭpāṭṭiṉ payaṉāka ki.pi. 300 ām āṇṭil mūlavarmaṉ marapiṉar āṭciyiṉ kīḻkuṭē āṭci eḻuntatu. itu pallava maṉṉaṉāl iksavāku nāṭṭai viṭṭu viraṭṭappaṭṭatiṉ payaṉākum. itē karuttuppaṭi mēṟkuc cāvakattil tarumaṉ irājyam vīḻntatu. intiya cakkaravartti camuttirakuptaṉāl cālaṅkāyaṉa maṉṉaṉ tōṟkaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.  ki.pi. 518 āṇṭukku muṉ pālampāṅ irācciyam (paḻaiya malaiyu pōli allatu mā - li) tiṭumeṉat toṭarpaṟṟatu. kauṇṭiṉiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ kīḻ viṣṇukuṇṭiṉ eṉṟa āntira aracaṉāl viraṭṭappaṭṭu nāṭukaṭattappaṭṭa (pautta) kāntāri mattiya cāvakattil 609 āṇṭukaḷukkuppiṉ 616 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ pōli allatu pāṭi eṉṟa irācciyattai nilaināṭṭiṉāṉ. itaṉ palaṉākak kaliṅkar āṭciyiṉar cāvakattaik kaippaṟṟiṉar. kaṅkā - kaliṅkar āṭci celuttiṉāṉ. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai intiyāviluḷḷa kaliṅkattiliruntu vanta cañcayaṉiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ malakkāvil kaliṅka āṭcikku aṭikōliṉar eṉṟu kaliṅkāviṉ, cīṉa nakal piratikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
724 - 8ām āṇṭil keṭā (kaṭāram) vīḻcci aṭaintatu. ataṉ palaṉāka akatikaḷ eṅkum malintaṉar. cañcayaṉ cailēntira rōṭu kūṭi akatikaḷukku mattiya cāvakattil nalla uṟaiviṭam kaṇ ṭāṉ. itaṉmūlam cailēntira maṉṉarkaḷ taṅkaḷukku muṉpōnta paṉaṉkāraṉ cāṇṭikkalacāṉaik kaṭṭiyatupōl 778ām āṇṭil cāṇṭimeṇṭ eṉṟa niṉaivuc ciṉṉattaik kaṭṭi muṭittaṉar.
caṉ - pō - ṭsiyiṉ āṭci cōḻarōṭu pōrāṭi 1023 - 24; 1030 ām āṇṭukaḷil taṟkālikamākat tōlviyuṟṟu mīṇṭum talai tūkkiyatu. atupōl erlaṅkā mīṇṭum talaitūkkavum cailēntirar āṭciyai akaṟṟic cāvakattil amaitiyaiyum oṟṟumaiyaiyum nilaināṭṭiṉāṉ.
ivaikaḷaiyellām mōyaṉ tam ārāycciyil mika virivāy ārāyntu viḷakkiyuḷḷār.1
ivvāṟu ulāntā, pirañcu, iṅkilāntu mutaliya nāṭṭi luḷḷa pēraṟiñarkaḷ palar cāvaka nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟai naṉku ārāyntu pala kaṭṭuraikaḷaiyum nūlkaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar. mēlum inta nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa putaiporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟaiyiṉaic cērnta aṟiñarkaḷ nāṭu muḻuvatum kāṭu malaikaḷai yum tēṭittirintum pūmiyai akaḻntu ārāyntum kiṭaitta palvēṟu kaṭṭiṭap pakutikaḷaiyum cilaikaḷaiyum aṇikaḷaiyum pāttiraṅkaḷaiyum āyutaṅkaḷaiyum taṅkaḷ putaiporuḷkaḷ cēkarittu vaittirukkum kaṇkāṭcic cālaiyil vaittu ārāyntu pala nūlkaḷaiyum tuṇṭup piracuraṅkaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
inta ārāyccit tuṟaiyil paṅku koṇṭavarkaḷ peritum ayal nāṭṭārkaḷāy irukkiṉṟārkaḷē yoḻiya intiyar kaḷō, tamiḻakarkaḷō, intōṉēciyarkaḷō atikamākak kāṇap paṭāmal iruppatu nām cintikka vēṇṭiya toṉṟākum.

11. cāvakap pāvaikkūttu
tamiḻakattil cumār 50 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ varai intap pāvaikkūttukkaḷ eṅkum mikap paravalāka naṭaipeṟṟu vantaṉa. kirāma makkaḷ iravellām viḻittiruntu intap pāvaikkūttaip pārttu makiḻntu vantaṉar. intap pāvaik kūttu niḻaṟpaṭam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum pommaikaḷai vaittu āṭṭuvataip pomma lāṭṭam eṉṟum pukalvar. intap pāvaikkūttu terukkūttu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. oru periya teruviṉ ōrattil oru ciṟiya koṭṭakai pōṭappaṭṭirukkum. muṉpakkam oru veḷḷait tuṇi kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. uḷḷē oru oḷimikka viḷakku eriyum. kūttai naṭattupavar tayār ceyyappaṭṭirukkum kataikkuriya paṭaṅkaḷaik kaiyil vaittuk koṇṭu ataṉ niḻal tuṇiyil piratipalikkumpaṭi ceyvar. paṭaṅkaḷiṉ kīḻē nīṇṭirukkum kuccukaḷaip piṭittuk koṇṭu paṭaṅkaḷai iyakkuvar. avarkaḷ kaikaḷō avarkaḷ iruntu iyakkuvatō, pārkkum makkaḷukkut teriyātavāṟu aṭippakkam karupputtuṇi kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. intak kūttarkaḷ kaikaḷil palapaṭaṅkaḷai orē camayattil vaittu iyakkuvar. paṭaṅkaḷiṉ kaikaḷum, kālkaḷum ēṉ? talaikaḷ kūṭa iyaṅkum. paṭaṅkaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉumākak koṇṭupōkap paṭuvatu mūlam peritākavum ciṟitākavum kāṇappaṭum. pōrkaḷ, tirumaṇaṅkaḷ mutaliya nikaḻccikaḷ ellām atikac ciṟappuṭaṉ kāṭṭappaṭum. iḵtu iṉṟaiya ciṉimāp paṭam pōṉṟatāka irukkum. ciṉimā vantu intiyāvil intak kalaiyai aṭiyōṭu aḻittuviṭṭatu.
cāvakattil pāvaikkūttum pommalāṭṭamum iṉṟum ciṟappuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. intak kūttaraṅkaṅkaḷ palavita amaippukkaḷai uṭaiyaṉavāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. mikat toṉmai yāṉavai vāyaṅpepar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. niḻalāṭṭattai vāyaṅ purvar eṉṟum pommalāṭṭattai vāyaṅkeṭāk vāyaṅ kiḷiṭṭik eṉṟum kūṟuvar. intak kataikaḷ perumpālum  cāvaka varalāṟṟait taḻuvi intiya itikācaṅkaḷāṉa irāmāyaṇam pāratamum mutaliyavaikaḷōṭu ārampamāki macāpākit maṉṉar kaḷōṭu muṭivaṭaiyum. intap pāvaikkūttu cīṉa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭatā allatu intiya nāṭṭiliruntu koṇṭu collappaṭṭa kalaiyā eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷāl aṟuti yiṭṭu uraikka muṭiyavillai. āṉāl nammaip poṟuttavaraiyil tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka intap pāvaikkūttu intiya mūlattai uṭaiya teṉṟu karutukiṟōm. pommalāṭṭam pommaikaḷaik kayiṟ ṟāl kaṭṭi mēliruntu iḻuppatumūlam pommaikaḷai iyakku vatākum.
iṉṟu ulakameṅkum makkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷaik kavarntu niṟpatu oḷi uruvappaṭaṅkaḷēyākum (ciṉimāvēyākum). muṟkālattil vāḻnta tamiḻmakkaḷ intap paṭakkāṭcikkup patilāka nāṭakaṅ kaḷaiyē naṭatti vantaṉar. avaikaḷaik kūttu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. nāṭakattil āṭalum pāṭalum iṇaintiruntaṉa. nāṭaka vaḷarcci yil tuṇaiyākap pala icaik karuvikaḷ iṇaikkappaṭṭaṉa. nāṭaka araṅkukaḷ nākarika muṟaiyil eḻuntuḷḷaṉa. nāṭakattai niṟai vuṭaiya tākkap palavaṇṇaṅkaḷ tīṭṭiya taṭṭikaḷum tiraiccīlai kaḷum toṅkaviṭappaṭṭaṉa. tiraiccīlaikaḷaip paḻantamiḻar eḻiṉi eṉṟaṉar. inta eḻiṉi, orumuka eḻiṉi, porumuka eḻiṉi, karantuvaral eḻiṉi eṉṟu mūṉṟa vakaippaṭṭatāy iruntatu. intat tiraikaḷil orupakkam orumuka eḻiṉiyum, maṟṟoru pakkam porumuka eḻiṉiyum, mēlē karantuvaral eḻiṉiyum irukkum. orumuka eḻiṉi eṉpatu orupakkamiruntu maṟṟoru pakkam varai taḷḷakkūṭiya tirai. porumuka eḻiṉi eṉpatu iraṇṭākap pirikkappaṭṭu iru pakkaṅkaḷiliruntum taḷḷakkūṭiya tirai. karantuvaral eḻiṉi mēlē maṟaivāyc cuṟṟivaikkappaṭṭirukkum tirai. itaṉaic curuḷ cañcikai eṉṟu peruṅkatai kūṟukiṟatu. araṅkiṉ uḷḷē aṇi aṇiyāy aḻakiya nilai viḷakkukaḷ ēṟṟappaṭṭirukkum. avaikaḷ mikunta nuṇṇaṟivuṭaṉ viḷakkiṉ niḻal āṭupavarkaḷ mītō kāṭciyaik kāṇavantiruppavarkaḷ mītō viḻātapaṭi takka iṭattil vaikkappaṭṭirukkum.
ēṟṟa vāyil iraṇṭuuṭaṉ poliyat
tōṟṟiya araṅkil toḻutaṉar ēttap
pūtarai eḻuti mēlnilai vaittut
tūṇ niḻal puṟappaṭa māṇviḷak keṭuttāṅku
orumuka eḻiṉiyum porumuka eḻiṉiyum
karantuvaral eḻiṉiyum purintuṭaṉ vakuttāṅku
ōviya vitāṉattu uraipeṟu nittilattu
mālait tāmam vaḷaiyuṭaṉ nāṟṟi
viruntupaṭak kiṭanta varuntoḻil araṅkattu					(cilap. araṅ. 105-113)
eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟukiṟatu. eṉavē mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē tamiḻakattil nāṭakakkalai ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyiruntatu eṉpataṟku uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu. antap paḻaṅkālattilē viḷakkiṉ veḷiccattil uruvaṅkaḷ tōṉṟuvataiyum peri tāvataiyum aṟintiruntaṉar. atōṭu nāṭakattil varum makkaḷ katāpāttiraṅkaḷait tavira āṭumāṭu, yāṉai, puli, ciṅkam, kuraṅku, kaḻuku, mayil, puṟā, karuṭaṉ, pāmpu pōṉṟavaikaḷaip piṭittu araṅkattilē koṇṭu varamuṭiyāmal iruntatāl palakaikaḷil allatu tōlkaḷil vilaṅkukaḷ allatu paṟavaikaḷ uruvaṅ kaḷait tīṭṭi vaṇṇamūṭṭi atai iyakki vantuḷḷaṉar.
nāṭakam maṉṉarkaḷiṉ ātaravu peṟṟa ōr uyarnta kalaiyāka miḷirntatu. kōyilkaḷil nāṭaka araṅkukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭu atil periya aṟiñarkaḷ nāṭakaṅkaḷil naṭittu vantaṉar. maṉṉarkaḷ ataik kaṇṭu cuvaittu naṭikarkaḷukkup pala paricukaḷ aḷittu vantaṉar.
āṉāl pāvaikkūttu (niḻalāṭṭam), pommalāṭṭam eṉṉum kalaikaḷait tamiḻakattiṉar attuṇai uyarnta kalaiyāka matikkavillai. pāmara makkaḷiṉ kalaiyākavum, ciṟṟūrkaḷil ēḻaimakkaḷ cuvaikkum kalaiyākavum iruntatu. itu ciṟṟūrkaḷil terukkōṭiyil celaviṉṟi oru ciṟu mēṭai kampukaḷāl amaittuc cuṟṟilum mūṉṟu puṟamum mēṟpuṟamum teṉṉaṅ kīṟṟiṉāl maṟaikkappaṭṭu muṉpakkam oru paḻaiya veḷḷait tuṇiyaik kaṭṭi uḷḷē viḷakkēṟṟi vaittuk koṇṭu tōliṉālāṉa uruvaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam muṉpakkamuḷḷa tuṇiyil niḻalākak kāṭṭuvārkaḷ. itaṉvāyilākap peruṅkataikaḷum mikac culapamāka makkaḷukkuk kāṭṭappaṭum. makkaḷ viṭiyum varai ayarātu kaṇṭukaḷippar. itu makkaḷiṉ terukkūttukkaḷil oṉṟāka matikkappaṭṭu vantatu. aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ varai viḷakka mākac colvatāṉāl ciṉimāk kāṭci talaittūkkum varai ikkalai tamiḻ nāṭeṅkum paravi iruntatu. ciṉimāk kāṭciyiṉ mūlam nāṭakak kalaiyum pāvaikkūttum pommalāṭṭamum kāṭēṟiviṭṭaṉa.
intap pāvaikkūttai iyakkum cūttiratāriyiṉ tiṟamai viyakkattakkatu. intac cūttiratāri kayiṟṟai iḻuttu iyakkuvataṉ mūlam kūttu muḻuvatum naṭai peṟukiṟatu. ivaṉatu iyakkun tiṟamai pōṟṟattakkatu. intak kalaiyil oru kuṭumpam muḻuvatumē īṭupaṭum. icai uraiyāṭal palavitamāṉa olikaḷaik kiḷappal mutaliyavaikaḷukku oru kuṭumpattil uḷḷa kaṇavaṉ, maṉaivi, makkaḷ, aṇṇaṉ, tampi mutaliya aṉaivarum īṭupaṭṭu vantārkaḷ. kirāma makkaḷ ūril varivacūlittup pāvaikkūttukkārarkaḷukku utavi vantaṉar. uṇavu uṭai mutaliya aṉpaḷippukaḷum aḷittu vantaṉar.
pāvaikaḷ āṭṭuttōl, erumaittōl, melliya palakai pōṉṟavaṟṟāl amaittu avaikaḷukkēṟṟa vaṇṇantīṭṭi āṭaikaḷum aṇikaḷum poliyak kalaittiṟamaiyōṭu naṭatti vantaṉar.
āntira nāṭṭil uḷḷa nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷ pāvaik kūttiṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷāka neṭuṅkālamāka iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. kumāracampavam, uttara harivamcam, iraṅkanāta irāmāyaṇam, pala nāṭṭiṉ vīracarittiram, campōpākkiyāṉam, pāñcālipariṇayam eṉṉum teluṅku nūlkaḷ itaippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. intak kūttaic civarāttiri viḻāvil ciricailam pōṉṟa kōyilkaḷil naṭatti ṉārkaḷ eṉṟum makāpāratam pōṉṟa kāviyaṅkaḷiliruntu eṭutta kataikaḷē itaṉ karuvāka iruntaṉa eṉṟum pālkuṟiki cōmanātar iyaṟṟiya paṇṭitārātya carittiram terivikkiṟatu.
āntira nāṭṭilum karnāṭaka nāṭṭilum cūtrata kompē (niḻalāṭṭam) cakkalat kompē (pommalāṭṭam) eṉpavaṟṟiṉ nilaikkaḷamāka yaṭcakāṉam eṉṟa icai nāṭakam upayōkap paṭuttappaṭukiṟatu. irunāṭukaḷilum yaṭcakāṉa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ vaḷamuṭaiyaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa.
uṇmaiyil itaṟkākavē teluṅkuk kavikaḷ yaṭcakāṉaṅ kaḷai eḻutiṉārkaḷ. mariṅkaṇṭi paṭṭaru irāmanucāccāriyār (ki.pi. 1850) iyaṟṟiya ciri irāma nāṭakam eṉṟa yaṭcakāṉam pommalāṭṭa irāmāyaṇam eṉṟu pukaḻ aṭaintirukkiṟatu.
āntira nāṭṭil neṭuṅkālamāka inta āṭṭam kuṭumpak kalaiyāki viṭṭatu. irāyala cīmā neṭuṅkālamākap pāvaikkūt tāṭum kuṭumpattiṉarukku nilaikkaḷamāka iruntu varukiṟatu. attakaiya kuṭumpattiṉar maṉṉarkaḷiṭam paricai peṟṟārkaḷ; iṉām kirāmaṅkaḷai aṭaintārkaḷ. irāyala cīmaiyil pomma lāṭṭam eṉṟa peyarōṭu ōr ūrē irukkiṟatu. aṅkē pala kaviñarkaḷ iruntu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷil oruvarākiya pommalāṭṭa sāmpaiyā eṉpavar (ki.pi. 1710) teluṅkil kirātārccu nīyam eṉṟa yaṭcakāṉattaiyum kaṉṉaṭattil iraṇṭu yaṭcakāṉaṅkaḷaiyum iyaṟṟiṉār.
āntira nāṭṭil uḷḷa pommalāṭṭakkārarkaḷ makārāṣṭira tēcattiṉiṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ kākatīyap pēraraciṉ ātaravil kalaiyiṉpam vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. cilar teliṅkāṉāvilum cilar irāyala cīmaiyilum āntira tēcattuk kaṭaṟkaraiyōrap pakutikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ. cilar kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiṟkum cilar kēraḷa nāṭṭiṟkum ceṉṟārkaḷ. jāvā mutaliya tīvukaḷukkuc ceṉṟavarkaḷum uṇṭu. avarkaḷālētāṉ pomma lāṭṭam jāvāvil vaḷarntu varukiṟatu pōlum eṉṟa, karuttup paṭa ṭākṭar e.vi. jōkarāv avarkaḷ āntira nāṭṭiluḷḷa tirivēṇi pattirikaiyil virivāka oru kaṭṭurai eḻutiyuḷḷār.1
iḵtaṉṟi nīṇṭakālamāka intiyāvil pāvaikkūttu eṉṉum niḻalāṭṭam makkaḷukku makiḻcciyūṭṭak kūṭiyatākavum nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷiṉ kalaiyākavum iruntu vantuḷḷatu eṉṟu ē.pi. kīt pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. ataṉōṭu mika vaḷarcciyaṭaintu nāṭeṅkum paravi niṉṟa nāṭakak kalaikku niḻalāṭṭam aṭippaṭaiyāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟum karutukiṟārkaḷ. kiṟitava ūḻi eḻuntu āyiram āṇṭiṟku appāl tāṉ intiyāvil piṟantu vaḷarnta inta niḻalāṭṭam eṉṉum kalaiyē veḷināṭṭiṟkup pōyirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. cīṉanāṭṭilum ikkalai paravi iruntatāka aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉ ṟārkaḷ. cāvakat tīviṟku intiyāviṉiṉṟē inta niḻalāṭṭak kalai ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉac cila aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟārkaḷ. mukamūṭi aṇintu āṭum oru pēyāṭṭam cāvakattil uṇṭu. cīṉattilum ilaṅkaiyiluṅkūṭa iruntatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. intiyāvil intak kalaiyiruntatu eṉṟu colvataṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ ituvarai kiṭṭavillai. ataṉāl itaṉōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuḷḷa pommalāṭṭam, niḻalāṭṭam (pāvaikkūttu) eṉṟa kalaikaḷ intiyāviṟkuriyaṉavalla eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. yār eṉṉa kūṟiṉum cāvakattil nilaviya pāvaikkūttu intiya mūlattai uṭaiyatu eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamillai. ēṉeṉil irāmakātaiyum pārata itikācamum icaiyum itaṟku uyirāka iruppatai niṉaikkum poḻutu namatu uḷḷak kiṭakkai cari eṉpatai meyppippatāka irukkiṟatu. eṉavē intiyarkaḷ intiyāviliruntu cāvakattiṟkup pauttam, caivam ākiya camayaṅkaḷōṭu icaiyaiyum koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. irāmā yaṇam pāratam pōṉṟa itikācaṅkaḷaiyum itai viḷakkum ciṟpakkalaiyaiyum, pommalāṭṭam eṉṟa putukkalaiyaiyum koṇṭu pōṉārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvatil viyappaṭaivataṟ koṉṟum illai. aṟiñar āṉanta kumāracāmi paṇṭaik kālattil intiyāvil pāvaikkūttukkaḷ (pommalāṭṭaṅkaḷ) iruntaṉa eṉpataṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷ illai eṉṟu karutukiṟārkaḷ. avar tam karuttai ēṟkum aṟiñar ji. cēkkap eṉpavariṉ karuttaiyum mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟār. cāvaka niḻaṟ paṭaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ tōliṉāl ceyyappaṭṭu iyaṅkakkūṭiya karaṅkaḷ, kālkaḷ, kaḻuttukkaḷ iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl avaikaḷ cīṉāvaip pōl oḷivīcukiṟatillai. parmā, tāylāntu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil ellāk kāṭcikaḷum iyaṟkai nilakkāṭci yōṭu oṉṟittu acaiya muṭiyātatāy amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa. cāvaka niḻaṟpaṭam uruvaṅkaḷ kuṟippukkaḷōṭu kaiyiṉāl iyakkappaṭu kiṟatu. niccayamākap pāvaikkūttu oru poḻutu pōkku viḷai yāṭṭu eṉpatai viṭa uyarntatāka matikkappaṭak kūṭiyatu ākum. paḻaiya iṉattavarkaḷiṉ apimāṉattil iḵtu ōr oḻuṅku muṟai yāṉa caṭaṅkāka irukkiṟatu. avarkaḷiṉ uṇarcci tōlpommai yākap piratipalikkiṟatu. uṇmaiyāṉa pommalāṭṭam (Wayang Golek) kaikaḷum kālkaḷum naṉṟākavum iyaṅkak kūṭiyaṉavākap parmāvil illāta vitamāy uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṟutiyāka, uyiruṭaṉ irukkum naṭikarkaḷ paṅku koḷḷum viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷum kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. mukamūṭi viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ (Wayang Topeng) uyarnta paḻaimaic ciṉṉamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. oḻuṅkāṉa araṅkaṅkaḷiṉ āṭṭaṅkaḷ (Wayang Wong) niḻaṟpaṭaṅ kaḷiṉ (pommalāṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ) pōliviḷaiyāṭṭākum. inta māṉuṣīka araṅkam mukkiyamāka eṭṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ uyar kulattiṉarāl uṟpatti ceyyappaṭṭatākum. āṉāl itaṉ tuṟaikaḷ ellām paḻaiya mūlaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu uruvāṉavai. pirapuk kaḷiṉ āṭaikaḷ, kāṭcikaḷ, paramparai nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷ, apinayaṅkaḷ ellām toṉmaiyāṉa mukattōṟṟattilēyē nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. intap paḻam poruḷkaḷ ellām varalāṟṟu rītiyāka illā viṭṭālum niccayamāka uḷḷattāl nēraṭiyāka uṇarappaṭum uṇmaiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. cāvaka araṅkaṅkaḷil itu uyir uḷḷatum uṇarccivacamāka aṟivuṟuttum vīrattaiyum kātalaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭum paṭamākavum irukkiṉṟatu. nilaiyāṉa naṭikar kuḻukkaḷ yōkkiyakarttāvum curakarttāvum aracavaiyāl ātarikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa. aracakuṭumpattil uḷḷavarkaḷ kūṭa itil aṅkam  peṟṟiruntaṉar. cila mukkiya campavaṅkaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa naṭikarkaḷ mātak kaṇakkāyp payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭu vantaṉar. cāvaka araṅkaṅkaḷ ātmīka uṇarcciyaiyum kalaip paṇpaiyum vaḷarkkum uyariya ciṉṉaṅkaḷāka iruntaṉa. itaṉōṭu cappāṉi luḷḷa nōkākku (No - gaku) oṉṟē oppiṭaṟkuriyatākum.
cāvakattiṉ nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷ nalla araṅkil naṭaipeṟu kiṉṟaṉa. ciṟappāka naṭikarkaḷiṉ nāṭṭiyat taṉmai araṅkiṉmītu mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟum poḻutē kāṭṭappaṭṭu viṭukiṟatu. itu tavira araca camūkattil āṭuvataṟkāka irukkum peṇkaḷ cerimpil, peṭōyō nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷai āṭuvatil kaitērntavar kaḷāka irukkiṟārkaḷ. aṉēkamāka araṇmaṉai nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷ aṉaittum alaṅkāra vakaiyākavē irukkum apinayaṅkaḷ kāṭṭum muṟaikaḷ ellām tamiḻakap pāṇiyait taḻuviyaṉa vākavē irukkum. paḻaiya cilaikaḷaip piratipalikkum muṟaiyil āṭum nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷ kuṟaivākavē irukkum.
cāvaka nāṭṭut tēcīyap pāṇiyil uḷḷa pañcāṭai pāṭṭik (Batik) eṉpatu tamiḻaka mūlattai uṭaiyatu eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamiṉṟu. perumpālāṉa āṭaikaḷiṉ alaṅkāra vēlaip pāṭukaḷ cāvakattil ilāmiya āṭci eḻumuṉ iruntavaiyē yākum. ēṉaiyavaikaḷ ciṟappāka vaṭṭamākap pūvēlai ceyyap paṭṭavaikaḷākum. avaikaḷ cāṇṭicivō veḷiccuvarkaḷmītu alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa pūvēlaippāṭukaḷaip piratipalippatāka iruntaṉa. mattiya cāvakattil nīlam, ūtā (Brown) ākiya iruniṟaṅkaḷ maṭṭum kāṇappaṭum. maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil civappum paccaiyum cērntirukkum. cātāraṇat tuṇṭukaḷ keyṉ pañcāṅ eṉa aḻaikkappaṭum. ivaikaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu āṇkaḷ aṇiyum āṭai pōṉṟu irukkum. itaik keyṉ cilaṇṭāṅ eṉpārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ mārpiṉmītu aṇiyum tāvaṇiyākavum upayōkap paṭuttappaṭum. talaiyil kaṭṭappaṭum oru caturat tuṇiyaik keyṉ kappālā eṉpārkaḷ. itu talaip pākaipōl maṭittut talaiyil aṇiyappaṭum. intat talaippākaiyaic cāvakattil talaiyil iṟukap piṇaittiruppārkaḷ. malēyāvilum, mēṟkuc cāvakatti lum cāram eṉṉum arai āṭaiyai āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum aṇivar. pālit tīvil pakaṭṭāṉa āṭaikaḷ (keyṉ praṭā) iḷavaracikaḷālum naṭikaikaḷālum aṇiyappaṭum. cāvaka āṭaikaḷ (paṭṭirik) poṟcarikaiyiṉāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭiruppatōṭu accum aṭikkappaṭṭirukkum. inta āṭaiyiṉ vēlaippāṭukaḷ cuttamāṉa acal intiyap pāṇiyākavē ilaṅkum. orucila āṭaiyiṉ alaṅkāra muṟaitāṉ cīṉattait taḻuvi irukkum paṭṭu mikak kuṟaivākavē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu.
 iḵtaṉṟi aḻakiya pañcāṭaikaḷ cumattirā tīviṉ ṭōpar pāṭṭāk eṉṟa iṭattil piṟpōkkuḷḷa cila iṉattavarkaḷāl neyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pōrṉiyōvilum iṉṉumpala ciṟu tīvukaḷi lum pañcāṭaikaḷ aḻakāka neyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷ ellām intiya pārampariyattuvam uṭaiyaṉaveṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ aṉaiva rālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cāvaka makaḷir mukaccāyalum naṭaiyum āṭaikaḷum nākarikamum paṇpum tamiḻakattōṭu mika neruṅki niṟpatai iṉṟum kāṇalām. cāvakarkaḷ maṅkōli marapiṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭālum tirāviṭa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ mukac cāyal avarkaḷiṭam peritum piratipalikkiṟatu. uṭaiyil naṭaiyil uṇavil kuṇattil tamiḻc cāyal naṉku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.

12. muṭivurai
tamiḻakam, makkaḷ iṉattiṉ toṭṭil eṉṟār cermaṉ nāṭṭiṉ maṇṇūl aṟiñar hekkal avarkaḷ. `ulakil mikat toṉmaiyāṉa moḻi tamiḻ, iṉṟum taṉ iḷamai kuṉṟātu kaṉṉit tamiḻ, eḻil peṟṟu ilaṅkukiṟatu. immoḻi piṟamoḻi yiṉ tuṇaiyiṉṟit taṉittiyaṅkavallatu eṉṟu pukaḻmālai cūṭṭiyuḷḷār kiṟitava camayap periya kuruvākiya irāparṭ kālṭuvel avarkaḷ. paḻantamiḻp pulavar kaṇiyaṉ pūṅkuṉṟaṉār avarkaḷ,
yātum ūrē yāvarum kēḷir	(puṟam. 192 : 1)
eṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
itaṉ mūlam, tamiḻakam ulaka makkaḷiṉ tāyakam; tamiḻ ulaka makkaḷukku urittāṉa toṉmai vāynta moḻi; tamiḻ makkaḷukku ulakaṉaittum contam; ulakiṉar aṉaivarum avarkaḷiṉ uṟaviṉar eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu.
inta eṇṇam - inta uṇarcci, tamiḻ makkaḷiṭam, aṟintō aṟiyāmalō uḷḷattil uṟaintu kiṭakkiṟatu. eṉavē avarkaḷ mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē ulakiluḷḷa ellā iṭaṅkaḷi lum ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. kaṭal kaṭantu palvēṟu tīvukaḷilum pōy vāḻntaṉar. appāl moḻikaḷum, nāṭukaḷum pirintu, niṟamum, aṟamum, paṇpāṭukaḷum vēṟupaṭṭa piṉṉarum tamiḻarkaḷ palvēṟu nilappakutikaḷukkum ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. tīvukaḷilum, tīpakaṟpaṅkaḷilum pōy vāḻntaṉar. aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyiṉar eṉṟa vuṇarvōṭu avarkaḷōṭu iṇaintu cakōtara nēyattōṭu vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷōṭu koḷviṉai koṭuppiṉai ceytu iraṇṭaṟak kalantu iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ camayattaiyum kalaikaḷaiyum paṇpāṭṭaiyum iṇaittu nallaṟam pēṇum nāṉila makkaḷāy vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭipukunta nāṭuka ḷilellām taṅkaḷ camayattaiyum kalaikaḷaiyum paṇpāṭṭai yum parappiṉar.
kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷ palavaṟṟil  kiṟitava cakāptam tuḷippataṟku muṉṉarē intiyarkaḷ - vaṅkarkaḷ, kaliṅkarkaḷ, āntirarkaḷ, kucarāttiyarkaḷ, tamiḻarkaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu ulaka varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷuḷ perumpālōr tirāviṭap peruṅ kuṭimakkaḷ ēṉ? avarkaḷilum ciṟappumikkavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷē. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟai ārāyntavarkaḷ avarkaḷ aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē naṉiciṟanta nākarikattaip paṭaittavarkaḷ; kaṭṭiṭak kalaiyil kai tērntavarkaḷ; āḻkaṭalil aḻakiya marak kalaṅkaḷil āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kal tolaiviṟkuc ceṉṟu palvēṟu nāṭukaḷaik kaṇṭavarkaḷ. ñāyiṟu oḷiyiṉaik kāṇātu marañ ceṭi koṭi mutaliyavaikaḷ aṭarntu putarkaḷāyk kāṭci aḷitta kāṭukaḷaiyellām  kaṇṭu, kāṭukaḷaintu nāṭukaḷākkiyum vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭiyum, kaḻaṉikaḷākat tiruttiyum putiya nākarikaṅkaḷai uruvākki yavarkaḷ. kiṟitu piṟappataṟku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē moḻikaḷaiyum ataṟku varivaṭivaṅkaḷaiyum vakuttavarkaḷ; nāṭu peyarntu cellat terintavarkaḷ eṉṟellām kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum tirāviṭarkaḷē ātiyil cumēriya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu intiyāviṟku vantu cintu āṟṟiṉ arukē kuṭiyēṟi aiyāyiram āṇṭiṟku muṉṉar mokañcatārō, arappā eṉṉum nakaraṅkaḷai eḻuppip paḻamperum nākarikaṅkaḷai vaḷarttavarkaḷ eṉṟu cintu natippakutiyil nilattai akaḻntu ārāyntu kaṇṭa airōppiyap pēraṟiñarkaḷ muṭipu kaṭṭiyuḷ ḷārkaḷ. cumēriya nāṭṭiluḷḷa `uruk eṉṟa iṭattil akaḻntu kaṇṭa pala poruḷkaḷukkum cintu veḷiyiṟ kaṇṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷukkum atikamāṉa orumaippāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉmūlam toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil `uruk nākarikattai uruvākkak kāraṇamāka irunta cumēriyarkaḷē intiyā pōntu intiyāviliruntu paṇṭaiya makkaḷōṭu kalantu vāḻntu vanta iṉattavarāka irukka vēṇṭum. antak kalappiṉ vaḻivanta makkaḷē cintuveḷi makkaḷāvār eṉṟu aṟiñar mekkēy avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.1
cumēriya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu paluccitāṉam, arappā, mokañca tārō mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷē tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉar.2 ivarkaḷ piṟkālattil āriyat tirāviṭap pōri ṉālō allatu iyaṟkaik kōḷāṟiṉālō aṅkiruntu peyarntu paṭippaṭiyākap pallāṇṭākat teṟku nōkkit tirumpi vantaṉar. ivarkaḷil cilar āṅkāṅkē taṅki nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷai amaittu vāḻntu vantaṉar. oru cilar teṟku nōkki vantu teṉkōṭiyil taṅkiṉar. ivarkaḷaiyē iṉṟu nām tamiḻar eṉkiṟōm.
camayam vantāl ivarkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭu nāṭu viṭṭu nāṭu ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟum paṇpu vāyntavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ evvitamāṉa taṭpaveppanilaikaḷaiyum poṟuttuk koṇṭu vāḻat terinta varkaḷ. āyiram  āyiram āṇṭukaḷāka uyarnta oppaṟṟa nākarikattai vaḷarttu vantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ cumēriya nāṭṭil uruvākkiya nākarikattaiviṭat talaiciṟanta nākarikattai arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum uruvākkiyavarkaḷ. appāl ivarkaḷ teṉ intiyāvil kuṭiyēṟi aṅku arappā, mokañcatārō nākarikattaiviṭac ciṟanta nākarikattai oppaṟṟa nākarittaik kaṭṭi vaḷarttavarkaḷ. intap paṇpu vāynta makkaḷ īḻam, malēyā, kaṭāram, ciṅkapuram, tāylāntu, kampōcakam, campā, pōrṉiyō, pāli, cāvakam, cumattirā pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷilellām pōntu ulaka mētaikaḷ kaṇṭu viyakkum oppaṟṟa nākarikattai uruvākkiya varkaḷ. iyaṟkaiyiṉ kōpattāl ivarkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷil vaḷartta naṉiciṟanta nākarikaṅkaḷ iṉṟu nalivuṟṟukkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. pallāṇṭākat tamiḻ nāṭṭil eḻunta araciyal viḷaivukaḷāl tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ iṉattavarkaḷ vāḻum cāvakam pōṉṟa nāṭ ṭōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koḷḷa muṭiyāmaṟ pōyviṭṭatu.
tamiḻarkaḷ palvēṟu nāṭukaḷil kuṭiyēṟi irukkiṉṟārkaḷ. eṉṟālum cāvaka nāṭṭiṉ kuṭiyēṟṟam māṇpu mikkatu. ulakil, tamiḻarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya nāṭukaḷil cāvakam talaiciṟanta kuṭiyēṟṟa mākum. avarkaḷ anta nāṭṭil kuṭiyēṟi annāṭṭu makkaḷōṭu pālil cīṉi karaivatupōl iraṇṭaṟa iṇaintuḷḷārkaḷ. ataṉōṭu taṅkaḷai annāṭṭumakkaḷiṉ moḻi, kalai, paṇpāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu iṇaittuk koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ. iṉṟu cāvakattil vāḻum makkaḷil yār annāṭṭiṉ paḻamaiyāṉa kuṭikaḷ? yār tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷ? eṉṟu evarālum pirittuk kāṇamuṭiyātavāṟu oṉṟittu vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. atēpōṉṟu tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kalai etu? tamiḻ nāṭṭu moḻi etu? tamiḻnāṭṭu nākarikam etu? eṉṟu pirittuk kāṇa muṭiyātavāṟu ellām cāvakattil iṇaintuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē tāṉ, ṭākṭar āṉantak kumāracuvāmi pōṉṟa kalaiviṟpaṉṉarkaḷ cāvakattil oḷirvatu intiyak kalaiyeṉṟō intōṉēciyak kalaiyeṉṟō intō - cāvakak kalaiyeṉṟō kūṟamuṭiyātu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. eṉavē nām cāvakattil vāḻum makkaḷai intō cāvaka makkaḷ eṉṟum aṅkuk kāṇum kalaikaḷai intō cāvakak kalai eṉṟō allatu tirāviṭa cāvakak kalai eṉṟō kūṟalām.
iṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭa teḷivāṉa muṭivu cāvakattil vāḻum makkaḷil perumpālōr tamiḻarkaḷōṭu iratta campantamāṉa toṭarpuṭaiya makkaḷākum. avarkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷ ellām caiva camayattait taḻuviyavarkaḷāvārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ kalaikaḷ ellām appaḻukkaṟṟa tamiḻnāṭṭuk  kalaikaḷiṉ piratipalippēyākum. oru kālattil cāvakattil tamiḻ aracāṅka moḻiyākak kūṭa iruntatu eṉṟu palar eṇṇukiṉṟārkaḷ. iṉṟum aṅkuḷḷōrkaḷiṟ cilar pallavar kuṭumpam, pāṇṭiyar kuṭumpam, cōḻar kuṭumpam, cērar kuṭumpam eṉṟu colli varukiṟārkaḷ.1 maturai pōṉṟa ūrkaḷum, caracuvatiyāṟu pōṉṟa natikaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ, vaṭamoḻic cācaṉaṅkaḷ pala mēlum mēlum kiṭaittu varukiṉṟaṉa. palvēṟu vitamāṉa kōyilkaḷum, niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum, civaperumāṉ, civaliṅkam, pārvati, koṟṟavai, tirumāl, makāviṣṇu, cattiyapāmā, urukkumaṇi, nāṉmukam, akattiyar (civakuru), kaṇapati, cūriyaṉ, varuṇaṉ, vāyu, kupēraṉ, irāvaṇaṉ, irāmaṉ, cītai pōṉṟa tiru uruvaṅkaḷ pala kallilum, cempilum, veḷḷiyilum poṉṉilum kiṭaittuḷ ḷaṉa. tamiḻar kaṇṭa nāṭṭiyattiṉ māṟṟuvaṭivu cāvakattilum kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟatu.2 peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum paṇṭaiyat tamiḻarkaḷaip pōla nēṟṟu varai araiyil āṭaikaḷ aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷakkaip pōṉṟa tiruviḷakkukaḷ aṅkuk kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. annāṭṭu āṇ peṇ makkaḷ tamiḻar kaḷaip pōl aḻakum cāntamum poṟumaiyum vāyntavarkaḷ. avarkaḷiṟ palar iṉṟu pauttarkaḷāyum, ilāmiyarkaḷāyum iruntālum tamiḻar paṇṭipāṭṭiṉiṉṟu māṟātavarkaḷāy nalla tamiḻarkaḷ pōl iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. ituvarai tamiḻarkaḷākiya nām namatu uṟaviṉarkaḷāṉa cāvaka makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koḷḷā malum avarkaḷ nāṭṭiluḷḷa caiva neṟiyaiyum avarkaḷiṉ kōyil teyvaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷaiyum kaṇṭu kaḷittu ārāyāma lum iruntuviṭṭōm. iṉiyāvatu tamiḻarkaḷ cāvaka nāṭṭiṟkuc camātāṉat tūtu cellavum kalait toṭarpu koḷḷavum araciyal naṭpai vaḷarkkavum muṉvara vēṇṭum.

1.	In Sumatra are found certain names of tribal subdivisions which are unmistakably South Indian, and specially Tamilian names such as Cholia Pandia Maelayala and also Pallava as well as Tekan (or Tekkanam or Deccan)- Early Tamil cultural influences in South East Asia S. J. Gunasegaran M.A. (Lond.)
2.	Java has had continuous contact with South India in later times. The Chola Empire in the 10th and the 11th centuries had close association with Java and Javenese culture was further influenced by Tamil Culture after the Cholas defeated the Sailendras of Java. Bhikkhus from Kanchipuram praise the Javanese ruler Hayam Wuruk in the 14th century Jayanagara adopted the characteristic Pandyan title Sundarapandya at his coronation in the 14th century and adopted the Pandyan emblem of the two fishes for his seal. - Early Tamil Cultural influences in South East Asia by S.J. Gunasegaran M.A. (Lond.) 1958- P. 14.
1.	“Some tribes among the head - hunting Dyaks in the wilds of Sumatra still call themselves Pandya, Chola Pellava and Maleyala. The great Sailendra rulers of Java and Sumatra who ruled a vast maritime empire from the seventh to the thirteenth Centuries with their Capitals at Srivijaya, modern palembang in Sumatra and Kadaram (Kedeah) to the north of Penang had the double - fish for their emblem like Pandyas of the extreme South of India.” Indian influence in the far East. by Prof. K.A. Neelakanta Sastri M.A. Page. 22
2.	“More extensive evidences of Indian Culture are found in middle of Java in the seventh century. This development may have been the result of long continued or of renewal immigration from South India” - History of India and Indonesian Art - by Ananda Coomaraswamy.

1.	Western Java remained independent and little is known of Central Java, but Majapahit controlled all eastern Islands, the Coastlands of Borneo, the Coastlands of Sumatra including Palembang and Malay Peninsula. Trade with China Indian Javanese products chiefly silk and cotton goods continued to flourish. In the Nagarakertagama Parapanca presents a vivid pricture of the walled city of Majapathit with its streets and palaces and of the manners and customs of its inhabitants. Entertainments are mentioned amongst others the Wayang Berber (exhibition of Scrol paintings with spoken text, and equivalent of the old Indian Yamapata exhibition as discribed in the Mudrarksasa) and Wayang Topeng, or masked dance, in which the King himself took part on the occasion of Straddha for the queen mother - History of Indian & Indonesian art - Ananda K. 1.	“The Rama reliefs which are found on the Hazara Ramaswami temple of Vijayanagar have been described as “beautifully exceuted and carved with great life and spirit” But how childish and insipid art those sculptures compared with the rendering of the same subject by the Javanese artist. Where as the
	Borobudur sculptures are supreme in rendering the Buddhist ideal of mental repose, those of Parambanan picture the heroic deeds of the divine Rama with great vigour and perfect lucidity. Mr. Havell which reproducing several of the Perambanan reliefs, rightly refers to them in terms of high praise” - The Relation between the art of India & Java by Dr. J.PH. Vogel. - London 1925.

1.	The Cumulative evidence which we have been able to adduce points to Southern India as the homeland of Indo - Javanese Culture. The inscriptions however mentioned in this Connection are all Brahmanical. If now we turn to the two earliest Buddhist inscriptions known to exist in Java it is surprising to find that they are written in a Character which has been described as an early type of Nagari and which decidedly originates from Northern India. Are we then to assume that the Hindu emigrants who introduced Buddhism into Java came from North, as the promoters of Brahmanism in all likelihood hailed from the South of the Indian contenent? - The Relation between the Art of Indian and Java - Dr. J.Ph. Vogel. London. 1925.
1. A young Javanese Scholar attached to the Leiden University has the very appropriate name of Sastrovidaghda - i.e., Skt. Sastrovidaghda, lit, “Versed in the Scriptures” - The Relation between the art of India and Java - by Dr. J.Ph. Vogel, London, 1925 P. 6-7
